## Heuristic / Inference Reanalysis And Validation

- File identity: `ClanDialogs.cpp` is a plausible organizational idea, but the best current inference is that the original modal code remained in the feature-owned Clan source. Evidence: contiguous Clan status/list/modal/bank code, shared opcode `0x4b` helpers and globals, detailed proposed-tree Clan rationale, all accepted owner routes, and the dated 2026-08-14 generated-output snapshot; the supervisor dynamically rereads the latest generated artifacts at gate time. Rejected alternative: force the modal island into `ui/dialogs` because one planning-tree line names it.
- Raw constructors: all nine starts contain normal constructor prologues, call a recovered base constructor, install the exact derived three-vtable family, return `this`, and end before alignment. Fresh xrefs and VA/RVA/raw-offset searches found no inbound route, but that negative entry evidence does not erase executable bytes. The six name/money derivatives are explicit `Derived(const unsigned char *packet) : ClanNameInputDialog(packet) {}` definitions, Enlist and Change are explicit `Derived(const unsigned char *packet) : ClanAttrInputDialog(packet) {}` definitions, and Leave is an explicit four-argument forwarding constructor to `AlertPane`. Live opener bodies are optimized inline expansions of the same source constructors.
- Method names: vtable slots, sibling EventHandler declarations, and direct base call `0x49e240` support `OnSubmitText`, `OnControlCommand`, `ValidateInputFields`, `HandlePointerOrMouseEvent(Event *)`, `OnSubmit`, `OnConfirm`, and `OnCancel`. In the dated 2026-08-14 type-catalog snapshot, `InputEvent` is absent and `Event` exists at size `0x110`; both handlers dereference and forward the same mutable pointer. These names are inferred/descriptive source spellings, not symbol-proven spelling, but they are substantially safer than raw IDA labels.
- Packet layouts: money variants emit `{0x4b, subtype, 1, amount32-be}`; name variants emit `{0x4b, subtype, 1, length, CP_ACP bytes}`; Enlist/Change submit inherited fields; Leave forwards boolean response. These are direct behavior facts and do not depend on file placement.
- Types/layout: the dated 2026-08-14 IDA UDT snapshot gives `ClanAttrInputDialog` size `0x870`, with `DialogPane` base bytes through `0x26c`, three `wchar_t[256]` fields at `+0x26c/+0x46c/+0x66c`, `unsigned short` at `+0x86c`, and two alignment bytes. The name-dialog family has no proven added persistent fields beyond its `0x26c` base shape.
- Resources: `aDl_14` and `aDl_13` are poor IDA labels for full `DLGCLAN1.EPF` and `DLGCLAN2.EPF` strings. Source should use literals at the consumer sites; IDA may safely use descriptive full-string labels.
- Header/source shape: declarations in CPP are a generated-source defect. Five affected declarations use H: `ClanAttrInputDialog`, `ClanEnlistInputDialog`, `ClanChangeInputDialog`, `ClanLeaveConfirmDialog`, and `ClanStatusPane`; method bodies remain child-owned CPP. The generated pair confirms all five channel repairs, and the limited ordinary-document callback synchronized the remaining `ClanAttrInputDialog.md` explanatory prose with the current formal state. Rejected alternative: leave declarations in CPP because generation currently compiles text there; that violates the project's formal channel rule and plausible source organization.
- Destructor support: modal classes rely on ordinary virtual destruction and base inheritance. Adjustor thunks, scalar-deleting wrappers, vtable arrays, and constructor-unwind cleanup remain compiler-generated; they must not become handwritten modal methods.
- Remaining uncertainty is lexical and historical, not behavioral: exact original filename and original spellings cannot be proven without artifacts absent from the executable. The best-supported realistic names and current feature-owned route are used rather than preserving IDA labels.

## Evidence Standards Used

- Direct evidence: IDA `server_health`, bounded `entity_query`, `lookup_funcs`, `analyze_batch`, `decompile`, `inspect_items`, `xref_query`, `find_bytes`, `get_bytes`, `get_comments`, and `type_query`; exact current files and hashes; validator-generated output readback.
- Corroborating evidence: by-file/class/memory/type documentation; matching executed B reports; current manual coverage; generated file coverage and research tracker; proposed source-tree detailed rationale.
- Negative evidence: zero function objects and zero inbound xrefs for all nine raw starts; zero exact VA, RVA, and mapped raw-offset dword patterns; no physical generated ClanDialogs source/header; no unique singleton/static/import/initializer owner for the proposed module. This evidence caps direct entry confidence and forbids unsafe IDA function creation, but does not justify omitting the source constructor definitions that reproduce the retained bytes.
- Evidence ladder: direct binary facts control behavior/ranges; class/vtable/caller context controls ownership; the dated 2026-08-14 generated and documentation graph snapshots support implementation placement, with current graph truth dynamically reread by the supervisor; source-history inference is accepted only when consistent with all three.
- Tool limitation in the dated 2026-08-14 evidence snapshot: IDA reported `auto_analysis_ready:false` while health was `ok` and Hex-Rays was ready. Bounded function, item, byte, xref, type, comment, and decompile requests returned coherent data; no result depended on a missing strings cache. That observed runtime identity is not asserted as current authority.

## Positive Evidence Summary

- The exact modal code and data islands are coherent: eighteen modeled methods, nine constructor bodies, eleven contiguous vtable groups, and two modal resource strings.
- All methods and classes already have accepted exact owners under `social/Clan.cpp`; the target's proposed file owns no unique state or source-bearing child.
- Fresh IDA xrefs reproduce class construction and virtual dispatch across status dispatchers, opener paths, raw constructors, and vtables.
- Physical generated output contains the source under Clan and no ClanDialogs artifact, matching the recommended non-standalone disposition.
- The strongest inference chain is: exact class/vtable identity plus feature-local caller/packet/resource coupling plus current owner graph plus generated readback. Together these outweigh one stale planning-tree filename.

## Ranked Ownership Analysis

### 1. UID0000I8 Clan / `NexusTK/social/Clan.cpp`

- Evidence for: complete current owner graph; physical generated output; feature-local status/list/modal/bank adjacency; shared Clan singleton, packet subtype family, resources, callers, and detailed proposed-tree rationale.
- Evidence against: the modal island is cohesive enough that a separate source file remains historically possible.
- Decision: accepted active source placement. Historical possibility does not justify a duplicate current source root.

### 2. UID0000IA ClanDialogs family/index with no path

- Evidence for: valuable all-family inventory, clear modal boundaries, coherent classes/resources/vtables, and a useful place to preserve the rejected split hypothesis.
- Evidence against: no unique source-bearing child, global, static, initializer, import boundary, generated artifact, or original filename proof.
- Decision: retain as a non-standalone family/index with owner/path `NONE`.

### 3. `NexusTK/ui/dialogs/ClanDialogs.cpp`

- Evidence for: one active-tree planning line and general UI-dialog organizational plausibility.
- Evidence against: Clan-specific protocols/singletons/callers; detailed source-tree section and all current owners use `social/Clan`; no output or artifact; a UI-layer move would split a tightly coupled feature family.
- Decision: rejected as the current reconstruction route; preserve as historical hypothesis only.

### Proposed new file/grouping, if applicable

- No new file is proposed. The grouping remains useful as UID0000IA documentation, while concrete source stays in Clan.
- Included: eleven modal classes, exact modal method/constructor evidence, modal vtables/layouts/resources, compiler-covered destructor implications.
- Rejected: ClanStatusPane packet parsers/dispatchers/openers as file ownership, all ClanBank/item dialogs, generic DialogPane/AlertPane support, singleton storage, and packet-buffer/socket implementations.

## Source Placement

- Recommended placement: class declarations in `NexusTK/social/Clan.h`; method definitions, including all nine retained out-of-line constructor definitions, in `NexusTK/social/Clan.cpp`; exact ownership remains on current by-class/by-memory pages through UID0000I8.
- This placement matches feature context and likely mid-2000s game-client organization: a broad feature module combining panes, modal flows, resources, and packet actions, with ClanBank as the supported larger split.
- Rejected placements: `ui/dialogs/ClanDialogs.cpp`, one file per modal class, PacketBuffer/Socket, ClanStatusPane ownership for class methods, and ClanBank for modal name/attribute dialogs.
- Remaining uncertainty: an original dedicated modal translation unit cannot be disproven absolutely, but current reconstruction must choose the highest-probability coherent source. The absence of direct artifact proof is not a reason to retain an active duplicate path.

### Explicit per-row classification and public endpoint binding

This matrix is a normative extension of each detailed T/I/P row above, not a replacement for it. The literal prestate, complete frame, dependency, permitted delta, protected state, invalidation condition, and expected readback in the detailed row remain mandatory. Each classification below is valid only when the supervisor's dynamic reread exactly matches that detailed prestate; any mismatch reclassifies that recommendation as `unsafe/stale/superseded`, and the proposed change must not be accepted. These endpoint-level clarifications do not change the mapped ledger/checklist claim, actor allocation, or verification state.

## Open Questions With Attempted Resolution

- Did an original `ClanDialogs.cpp` exist? Checked code/data boundaries, callers, globals, imports, initializers, source tree, owner graph, generated output, and reports. No direct proof exists. Best current resolution: do not reconstruct an independent file; preserve the hypothesis historically.
- Should raw constructors emit? Yes. Exact bounded disassembly resolves each range as an out-of-line delegating constructor definition with a recoverable source signature. Missing inbound entry references explain why IDA has no function object and cap confidence, but do not permit omission of retained executable bytes; each exact by-memory child must emit its constructor body.
- Is the dialog event type `InputEvent` by reference or `Event` by pointer? Current type lookup finds no `InputEvent`; `Event` exists at size `0x110`; both functions dereference `m_type`, forward the same mutable pointer, and call the canonical `DialogPane::HandlePointerOrMouseEvent(Event *)` implementation. Resolution: `bool HandlePointerOrMouseEvent(Event *event)` for ClanName, ClanAttr, and the ClanStatusPane secondary EventHandler declaration.
- Are `OnConfirm`/`OnCancel` integers because the dated 2026-08-14 IDA snapshot says `int()`? No. Both wrappers call the same decompiled void packet sender and ignore the implicit receiver; incidental EAX preservation is not a source return contract. Resolution: formal and safe IDA types are `void __thiscall ... (void *this)`.
- Can every challenged IDA row receive a source-quality type? Independently reproduced literal renderer outcomes support I01 and I03-I12. I01/I03-I07/I11-I12 use void-pointer receivers where their class UDTs are absent; I08-I10 use the exact existing `ClanAttrInputDialog` UDT. I01 and I08 normalize source-facing `const unsigned char *packet` to literal IDA `const unsigned __int8 *packet`; I06 and I07 render source-quality function types with `const wchar_t *text` while preserving physical frame rows as `lpWideCharStr/LPCWCH`. I02 alone must remain rename/comment-only because exact disassembly carries a live EBX input into a helper and ends with two-word cleanup while only one stack word is semantically named; its complete custom type and frame remain protected. No result creates a general alias waiver.
- Are source-facing names exact? No original symbols exist for several functions. Vtable/class/packet behavior supports realistic descriptive names. Use those names rather than IDA labels; cap confidence rather than leave reverse-engineering artifacts in final source.
- Should affected class declarations live in CPP? No. Physical formal/generated review identified the original four modal defects and the later `ClanStatusPane` defect. Project channel rules and plausible developer source require all five declarations in H with CPP limited to implementation/children; command `000000023656` verifies that result.
- Does by-file's blank tracker reconstructability require a missing metadata header? No. By-file schema does not expose `RECONSTRUCTABLE`; the correct operational disposition is path/owner `NONE` plus explicit non-standalone prose and manual coverage classification.
